# Gold set review sheet

Evidence behind each pipeline value. Verdicts go in `gold_set.csv` (`truth_*` columns).

## PGa01355e3c7: SUZETRIGINE / Acute Pain

- Sponsor Vertex Pharmaceuticals Incorporated; TA pain; cohort novel; first P3 2022-10-03; moiety first approved 2025-01-30; sample: suzetrigine / acute pain (known dates)
- **Label:** event 1.0, y_prog 1.0, approval 2025-01-30 (appl 219209), source drugsfda_orig+label / high, failure basis nan, CRLs -
- **Approved product** JOURNAVX (appl 219209); label indications: "1 INDICATIONS AND USAGE JOURNAVX is indicated for the treatment of moderate to severe acute pain, including postoperative pain, in adults. JOURNAVX is a sodium channel blocker indicated for the treatment of moderate to severe acute pain, including postoperative pain, in adults. ( 1 )"
- **Phase 3 trials:**
  - NCT05553366 [COMPLETED] 2022-10-03 -> 2023-12-15: Evaluation of Efficacy and Safety of VX-548 for Acute Pain After a Bunionectomy
  - NCT05558410 [COMPLETED] 2022-10-10 -> 2023-08-25: Evaluation of Efficacy and Safety of Suzetrigine for Acute Pain After an Abdominoplasty
  - NCT05661734 [COMPLETED] 2023-01-09 -> 2023-12-12: A Single-arm Study to Evaluate Safety and Effectiveness of VX-548 for Acute Pain
- **Levels:**
  - L1 2023-08-24 (day before first Phase 3 primary completion / topline)
  - L2 2024-02-06 (day after Phase 3 topline (press_release))
    - PHASE3_POSITIVE 2024-02-05 [filing_date] 0000875320-24-000012_ex-991_q42023.htm: "Vertex recently shared positive results from the three Phase 3 trials of VX-548 in acute pain."
  - L3 2024-08-02 (day after filing acceptance announced)
    - PDUFA_DATE 2024-08-01 [filing_date] 0000875320-24-000193_ex-991_q22024.htm: "— FDA accepted NDA for suzetrigine (VX-548) for moderate-to-severe acute pain with Priority Review and PDUFA target action date of January 30, 2025"
  - L5 2025-01-23 (7 days before PDUFA goal date 2025-01-30)
    - PDUFA_DATE 2024-08-01 [filing_date] 0000875320-24-000193_ex-991_q22024.htm: "— FDA accepted NDA for suzetrigine (VX-548) for moderate-to-severe acute pain with Priority Review and PDUFA target action date of January 30, 2025"
